# Fungicide Recommendations Extracted from the PDF

> Source transcriptions only, not independent product recommendations. Verify current local registration and the product label before use.
> Dosage values and waiting-period entries are copied from the source table; line breaks are collapsed to spaces. Dosage subfields follow source column order (active ingredient, formulation, dilution), and source units are not converted.
> Some rows are seed/tuber treatments and use per-seed units. The source has no separate application-instructions column; instruction-like text is retained under its original source field.
> “PDF page” uses the 1-based page index in the PDF file, not the printed footer number. A Potato / Black scurf row on PDF page 2 has no product heading visible and is marked unidentified rather than assigned by inference.
> Non-numeric text found in the source waiting-period column is retained verbatim and is not interpreted as a number of days.
